for c,a in zip(C,A):
 G=plus(G,mm(T(c),mm(R,c)))
 bm=plus(bm,mm(T(c),[[v for v in a]]*4))
Y=scale(F(-45,4),mm(inv(G),bm))
print("G positive definite",pd(G),"Y entries",list(map(str,sorted({x for r in Y for x in r}))))
K=[mm(c,Y) for c in C]
gamma=[F(1,10000)*max(sum(abs(v) for v in mm(T(B),k)[i]) for k in K) for i in range(3)]
print("B3 gamma",list(map(str,gamma)))

def host_date():
    plain = subprocess.run(["date"], capture_output=True, text=True, check=True).stdout.strip()
    iso = subprocess.run(["date", "+%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%S%z"], capture_output=True, text=True,
                         check=True).stdout.strip()
    return {"date": plain, "iso": iso}


def sha256_file(path):
    return hashlib.sha256(Path(path).read_bytes()).hexdigest()


def verdict(ok):
    return "MATCH" if ok else "MISMATCH"


def factor_int(n):
    """{p: e} with prod p^e == |n| (n != 0); FLINT factorisation, multiplied back as a check."""
    n = abs(int(n))
    assert n != 0
    if n == 1:
        return {}
    fac = {int(p): int(e) for p, e in flint.fmpz(n).factor()}
    back = 1
    for p, e in fac.items():
        assert flint.fmpz(p).is_prime()
        back *= p ** e
    assert back == n
    return dict(sorted(fac.items()))


def factor_frac(q):
    q = Fraction(q)
    fac = dict(factor_int(q.numerator))

Notation.  Coordinates k = 0,1,2,3 <-> (r,t) = (-1,-1), (-1,1), (1,-1), (1,1) (row/col -1 first,

entry sum 5 and all row/col sums >= 1, w_s = 5!/prod s_k!.  For mu in (0,inf)^4:
    d_s = w_s mu^s,  Phi = sum_s d_s,  m = sum_s d_s s,  S2 = sum_s d_s s s^T,  D = diag(m).
 [X] Phi equals the closed form (x+y+z+w)^5-(x+y)^5-(x+z)^5-(y+w)^5-(z+w)^5+x^5+y^5+z^5+w^5
     (inclusion-exclusion over the four empty-margin events; pairwise intersections leave one cell).
 [X] F1: mu_l dm_k/dmu_l = sum_s d_s s_k s_l = S2_kl   (Euler: mu_l d(mu^s)/dmu_l = s_l mu^s).
 [X] F2: sum_k s_k = 5 for every s  =>  S2 1 = 5 m,  sum_k m_k = 5 Phi,  m = mu * grad Phi (=(5.8)).
     F3: D 1 = m (trivial).
 [X] F4: S spans R^4 and d_s > 0  =>  S2 = sum_s d_s s s^T is positive definite (PD).

(a) Let theta = ln mu.  By F1, dm/dtheta = S2 (PD, F4); so theta(m) is smooth on the open cone
    {m(mu): mu > 0} (= interior of M0, Lemma 5.3) with dtheta/dm = S2^{-1} (symmetric).  Then
        grad psi = ln m/2 + 1/2 - theta - (dtheta/dm)^T m = ln m/2 + 1/2 - theta - S2^{-1} m
                 = ln m/2 - theta + 3/10          (S2^{-1} m = 1/5 by F2; this is paper (5.18)-(5.19)),
        Hess psi = D^{-1}/2 - dtheta/dm = D^{-1}/2 - S2^{-1}.
    VERDICT (a): correct.

(b) Slice {sum m = c}: tangent space U = 1^perp.  Scaled coordinates: put S^ = D^{-1/2} S2 D^{-1/2}
    (PD).  By F2, S^ sqrt(m) = D^{-1/2} S2 1 = 5 D^{-1/2} m = 5 sqrt(m): sqrt(m) is an EIGENVECTOR of
    S^, so V := sqrt(m)^perp is S^-invariant and (S^^{-1})|_V = (S^|_V)^{-1} exactly.
    u in U  <=>  u = D^{1/2} a with a in V;   v in m^perp  <=>  v = D^{-1/2} c with c in V.
        u^T Hess psi u = a^T (I/2 - S^^{-1}) a,     v^T S2 v = c^T S^ c,     v^T D v = c^T c.
    Hence, with r_1..r_3 the eigenvalues of S^|_V (= generalised eigenvalues of (S2 | D) on m^perp),
    the slice Hessian, taken w.r.t. the metric u^T D^{-1} u on U, has eigenvalues
        lambda_i = 1/2 - 1/r_i,     i.e.   r_i = 2/(1 - 2 lambda_i),
    and   slice Hessian negative definite (ND)  <=>  all r_i < 2  <=>  u^T S2 u < 2 u^T D u on
    m^perp \ {0}.  (The switch from 1^perp to m^perp is legitimate ONLY because S2 1 and D 1 are both
    parallel to m, i.e. because every spectrum has entry sum 5; for a general PD S^ the compression
    of the inverse dominates the inverse of the compression and only "=>" would survive.)
    VERDICT (b): correct, with a wording caveat: the exact equivalence is "slice Hessian ND at m(mu)
    <=> condition"; ND implies local strict concavity but not conversely, so "strictly concave at
    m(mu) iff" overstates the converse.  ND on the whole open (convex) slice => strictly concave there.

(c) Mpoly = 5 Phi (2D - S2) + 3 m m^T.  Mpoly 1 = 5 Phi (2m - 5m) + 3 m (5 Phi) = 0 [X].  For v in
    m^perp: v^T Mpoly v = 5 Phi v^T (2D - S2) v with Phi > 0.  Since <m,1> = 5 Phi > 0, every x is
    x = v + t 1 with v in m^perp, and x^T Mpoly x = v^T Mpoly v (Mpoly symmetric, Mpoly 1 = 0).  The
    coordinate hyperplane {x_k = 0} is a complement of span(1) for every k (1 has no zero entry), so
        (b)  <=>  Mpoly PD on m^perp  <=>  Mpoly PSD with kernel exactly span(1)

    sqrt(m), prod_i (2 - r_i) = det(2I - S^)/(2 - 5) = -det(2D - S2)/(3 prod m), so (*) is the

    VERDICT (c): correct.

  for j in range(n):

def det(a):

   588	The above argument is easily extended to work for higher moments, by counting the pairings
   589	that contain a given locally rainbow balanced 3-colouring and set of oriented cycles of the
   590	appropriate lengths. The contribution from cases where the cycles intersect turn out to be
   591	negligible, for the following reasons. Suppose that the cycles form a subgraph H with ν
   592	
   593	                                               11
   594	vertices and µ edges, and the total length of cycles is ν0 . Then in the case of disjoint cycles,
   595	ν = µ = ν0 . A factor of Θ(nν0 −ν ) is lost if there is a reduction in the number of vertices of
   596	H, compared with the disjoint case, because of the reduced number of ways of placing the
   597	cycles on the coloured vertices. Similarly, a factor Θ(nν0 −µ ) is gained in the function f for the
   598	reduction in the number of edges of H, because of the corresponding increase in the number of
   599	points to be paired up at the end. Thus, the contribution from such an arrangement of cycles
   600	to the quantity being estimated is of the order of nν−µ times that of the contribution from
   601	disjoint cycles. In all non-disjoint cases, H has more edges than vertices, since its minimum
   602	degree is at least 2, and it has at least one vertex of degree at least 3. There are only finitely
   603	many isomorphism types of H to consider, so the contribution from the case of disjoint cycles
   604	is of the order of n times the rest. The significant terms in this case decompose into a product
   605	of the factors corresponding to the individual cycles. Consequently, we obtain the following
   606	result, as required for (2.1) in accordance with (2.2):
   607	                                                        j              mk

   781	        fd_checks.append({"vT_Hf_v_exact": str(exact_q),
   782	                          "second_difference_mpmath": mpmath.nstr(fd, 25),
   783	                          "rel_err": mpmath.nstr(abs(fd - mpmath.mpf(exact_q.numerator) / exact_q.denominator)
   784	                                                 / abs(mpmath.mpf(exact_q.numerator) / exact_q.denominator), 5)})
   785	    res["item3_hessian"] = {
   786	        "derivation": "f(d) = sum d ln(w/d) + (1/2) sum m ln m, m = A d  =>  "
   787	                      "Hf = -diag(1/d) + (1/2) A^T diag(1/m) A",
   788	        "centre_checks": {
   789	            "all_m_equal_5/36": m_ok,
   790	            "all_n_ij_equal_1/9": nij_ok,
   791	            "w_v/b_v_equals_8100": wb_ratio,
   792	            "sum_e A[e][v] = 5": colsumA_5,
   793	            "gradient_at_b_is_constant_vector_so_b_is_stationary_on_the_affine_slice":
   794	                bool(m_ok and wb_ratio and colsumA_5 and ones_P0_zero),
   795	        },
   796	        "Hf_at_b": "Hf = -diag(8100/w_s) + (18/5) blockdiag_9(S S^T);  K5 := -5 Hf is integral",
   797	        "K5_block_diagonal": block_diag,
   798	        "detG_P0 (G = -5 P0^T Hf P0)": str(detG0),
   799	        "detG_PB (independent basis)": str(detGB),
   800	        "det_basis_independent (P0 vs PB)": detG0 == detGB,
   801	        "control_raw_nullspace_basis_det_equals_index^2_times_detG": raw_ratio_is_index_sq,
   802	        "control_raw_nullspace_basis_index": str(idx_raw),
   803	        "control_unconstrained_hessian_indefinite": {
   804	            "K5_diagonal_positive": K5_diag_pos,
   805	            "b^T K5 b (exact)": str(bK5b),
   806	            "indefinite_on_R^324": K5_diag_pos and bK5b < 0,
   807	            "meaning": "negative definiteness holds only on the constraint lattice; the Sylvester test "
   808	                       "on G = P^T K5 P is therefore not vacuous",
   809	        },
   810	        "detH_tilde_exact": {
   811	            "formula": "det H~ = det(P^T Hf P) = (-1)^301 det(G) / 5^301",
   812	            "sign": detH_sign,
   813	            "factorisation": fac_str(detH_fac, detH_sign),
   814	            "factorisation_json": fac_json(detH_fac),

   817	        },
   818	        "paper_detH": fac_str(PAPER["detH"], PAPER["detH_sign"]),
   819	        "detH_vs_paper": verdict(det_match),
   820	        "computed_over_paper": fac_str(det_ratio_to_paper),
   821	        "negative_definiteness_exact": {
   822	            "method": "Sylvester: Bareiss fraction-free elimination (no pivoting) of G = -5 H~ "
   823	                      "(P0 coordinates); leading principal minors Delta_k; LDL^T pivots D_k = "
   824	                      "Delta_k/Delta_(k-1); cross-checked against flint fflu",
   825	            "G_symmetric": sym_ok,
   826	            "all_301_leading_minors_positive": all_pos,
   827	            "last_minor_equals_det": last_eq_det,
   828	            "flint_fflu_no_permutation_and_same_minors": fflu_agree,
   829	            "min_LDL_pivot_of_G_(float_display)": float(min_piv),
   830	            "min_LDL_pivot_of_G_exact": str(min_piv) if len(str(min_piv)) < 200 else
   831	            f"{float(min_piv)!r} (exact fraction has {len(str(min_piv))} chars)",
   832	            "bareiss_seconds": round(t_bareiss, 2),
   833	            "fflu_seconds": round(t_fflu, 2),
   834	            "H_tilde_negative_definite": all_pos and last_eq_det and fflu_agree,
   835	            "verdict_vs_paper": verdict(all_pos and last_eq_det and fflu_agree),
   836	        },
   837	        "numeric_cross_check_float64": {
   838	            "eig_G_P0_min": float(ev_G0.min()),
   839	            "eig_G_P0_max": float(ev_G0.max()),
   840	            "restricted_Hf_orthonormal_basis_eig_max (basis independent)": float(ev_restricted.max()),
   841	            "restricted_Hf_orthonormal_basis_eig_min": float(ev_restricted.min()),
   842	            "slogdet_G_P0_sign": float(sign_ld),
   843	            "slogdet_G_P0_log": float(logabs),
   844	            "log_detG_exact_(display)": float(mpmath.log(mpmath.mpf(detG0))),
   845	        },
   846	        "numeric_cross_check_hessian_formula_mpmath_dps80": {
   847	            "method": "central second difference of f along b +- eps*P0 z, eps=1e-25, z in [-2,2]^301 "
   848	                      f"(seed {SEED}) vs exact v^T Hf v",
   849	            "f(b)_mpmath": mpmath.nstr(f_b, 30),
   850	            "trials": fd_checks,
   851	        },
   852	    }
   853	    timings["item3_total_s"] = time.perf_counter() - t0
   854	
   855	    # ------------------------------------------------------------------ item 4: g(b), prod w
   856	    t0 = time.perf_counter()
   857	    prod_w = math.prod(W)
   858	    prod_w_fac = factor_int(prod_w)
   859	    # g(b)^2 = prod_e m_e^(1/2) / prod_v b_v = (5/36)^18 * 8100^324 / (prod_s w_s)^9
   860	    g_sq = Fraction(5, 36) ** 18 * Fraction(8100) ** 324 / Fraction(prod_w) ** 9
   861	    # independent direct evaluation from the 36 m_e and 324 b_v: g^4 = prod_e m_e / prod_v b_v^2
   862	    prod_m = Fraction(1)
   863	    for me in m:
   864	        prod_m *= me
   865	    prod_b = Fraction(1)
   866	    for x in b:
   867	        prod_b *= x
   868	    g4_direct = prod_m / prod_b ** 2
   869	    g_sq_fac = factor_frac(g_sq)
   870	    g_is_rational = all(e % 2 == 0 for e in g_sq_fac.values())
   871	    g_fac = fac_scale(g_sq_fac, Fraction(1, 2))
   872	    g_match = fac_eq(g_fac, PAPER["g_b"])
   873	    res["item4_g_and_w"] = {
   874	        "prod_s_w_s": str(prod_w),
   875	        "prod_s_w_s_factorisation": fac_str(prod_w_fac),
   876	        "g_b_definition": "g(b) = prod_{ij,rt} m^(1/4) / prod_{ij,s} b^(1/2), m = 5/36, b = w_s/8100",
   877	        "g_b_squared_formula": "(5/36)^18 * 8100^324 / (prod_s w_s)^9",
   878	        "g_b_fourth_power_direct_equals_formula": g4_direct == g_sq ** 2,
   879	        "g_b_is_rational": g_is_rational,
   880	        "g_b_factorisation": fac_str(g_fac),
   881	        "paper_g_b": fac_str(PAPER["g_b"]),
   882	        "g_b_vs_paper": verdict(g_match),
   883	        "g_tilde_equals_g_at_b": "g~(b~) = g(b) because the parametrisation maps b~ to b "
   884	                                 "(centre_b_satisfies_parametrisation_exactly)",
   885	        "f_b_exact": {
   886	            "f(b)": "ln 8100 + (5/2) ln(5/36)  (sum b = 1, sum m = 5)",
   887	            "exp(2 f(b)) == (5^(5/2) * 25/24)^2": Fraction(8100) ** 2 * Fraction(5, 36) ** 5
   888	            == Fraction(5 ** 5) * Fraction(25, 24) ** 2,
   889	            "exp_f_b_float": float(mpmath.exp(f_b)),
   890	            "paper_value": "25/24 * 5^(5/2) ~ 58.2309",
   891	            "verdict": verdict(Fraction(8100) ** 2 * Fraction(5, 36) ** 5
   892	                               == Fraction(5 ** 5) * Fraction(25, 24) ** 2
   893	                               and abs(float(mpmath.exp(f_b)) - 58.2309) < 5e-5),
   894	        },
   895	    }
   896	    timings["item4_s"] = time.perf_counter() - t0
   897	
   898	    # ------------------------------------------------------------------ item 5: constants
   899	    t0 = time.perf_counter()
   900	    item5 = {}
   901	    # (a) delta_k from the transition matrix of Section 3 (matrix rebuilt from the state rule)
   902	    states = [(1, 2), (2, 1), (3, 1), (1, 3), (2, 3), (3, 2)]
   903	    c_alpha = Fraction((1 + 1) ** 3, (1 + 1) ** 5 - 1 - 1)             # 8/30
   904	    c_beta = Fraction((1 + 1) ** 3 - 1, (1 + 1) ** 5 - 1 - 1)          # 7/30
   905	    Mtr = [[Fraction(0)] * 6 for _ in range(6)]
   906	    for x, (a1, b1) in enumerate(states):
   907	        for y, (b2, c2) in enumerate(states):
   908	            if b2 == b1:
   909	                Mtr[x][y] = c_alpha if a1 != c2 else c_beta
   910	    paper_pattern = [[0, "b", 0, 0, "a", 0], ["b", 0, 0, "a", 0, 0], ["a", 0, 0, "b", 0, 0],
   911	                     [0, 0, "b", 0, 0, "a"], [0, 0, "a", 0, 0, "b"], [0, "a", 0, 0, "b", 0]]
   912	    paper_M = [[c_alpha if x == "a" else c_beta if x == "b" else Fraction(0) for x in rw]
   913	               for rw in paper_pattern]
   914	    Msp = sp.Matrix(6, 6, lambda a, c: sp.Rational(Mtr[a][c].numerator, Mtr[a][c].denominator))
   915	    lam = sp.Symbol("x")
   916	    charpoly = sp.factor(Msp.charpoly(lam).as_expr())
   917	    eig = {str(k): v for k, v in Msp.eigenvals().items()}
   918	
   919	    def delta(k):
   920	        return Fraction(1, 15 ** k) + 2 * Fraction(1, (-5) ** k) + 2 * Fraction(1, (-3) ** k)
   921	
   922	    def lam_k(k):
   923	        return Fraction(4 ** k, 2 * k)
   924	
   925	    trace_ok = True
   926	    Mk = [[Fraction(int(a == c)) for c in range(6)] for a in range(6)]
   927	    for k in range(1, 31):
   928	        Mk = [[sum(Mk[a][q] * Mtr[q][c] for q in range(6)) for c in range(6)] for a in range(6)]
   929	        tr = sum(Mk[a][a] for a in range(6))
   930	        if tr != Fraction(1, 2 ** k) * (1 + delta(k)):
   931	            trace_ok = False
   932	        # E(Y X_k)/EY -> (1/2k) 8^k Tr(M^k) = lambda_k (1 + delta_k)
   933	        if Fraction(8 ** k, 2 * k) * tr != lam_k(k) * (1 + delta(k)):
   934	            trace_ok = False
   935	    bound = Fraction(1, 225) + Fraction(2, 25) + Fraction(2, 9)
   936	    item5["a_delta_k_(2.2)"] = {
   937	        "source": "transition matrix rebuilt here from the state rule (Section 3) with "
   938	                  "c_alpha=(1+1)^3/30, c_beta=((1+1)^3-1)/30 (generating functions at the saddle x=1, "
   939	                  "taken from the paper's derivation); lambda_k = 4^k/(2k) taken from the paper/[17]",
   940	        "c_alpha": str(c_alpha), "c_beta": str(c_beta),
   941	        "matrix_equals_paper_printed_matrix": Mtr == paper_M,
   942	        "charpoly_factored": str(charpoly),
   943	        "eigenvalues_with_multiplicity": eig,
   944	        "Tr(M^k) == 2^-k (1+delta_k) and 8^k Tr(M^k)/(2k) == lambda_k (1+delta_k), k=1..30": trace_ok,
   945	        "delta_1": str(delta(1)),
   946	        "max_|delta_k|_k>=2_bound": str(bound),
   947	        "|delta_k|<=bound<1 for k=2..60": all(abs(delta(k)) <= bound for k in range(2, 61)) and bound < 1,
   948	        "verdict": verdict(Mtr == paper_M and trace_ok and delta(1) == -1 and bound < 1),
   949	    }
   950	    # (b) (2.3): exp(sum lambda_k delta_k^2) closed form
   951	    terms = [(Fraction(4, 225), 1), (Fraction(-4, 45), 4), (Fraction(-4, 75), 4),
   952	             (Fraction(4, 9), 4), (Fraction(4, 15), 8), (Fraction(4, 25), 4)]
   953	    expand_ok = all(lam_k(k) * delta(k) ** 2
   954	                    == Fraction(1, 2 * k) * sum(c * x ** k for x, c in terms) for k in range(1, 41))
   955	    prod_terms = Fraction(1)
   956	    for x, c in terms:
   957	        prod_terms *= (1 - x) ** c
   958	    paper_inner = (Fraction(221, 225) * Fraction(49, 45) ** 4 * Fraction(79, 75) ** 4 * Fraction(5, 9) ** 4
   959	                   * Fraction(11, 15) ** 8 * Fraction(21, 25) ** 4)
   960	    # exp(sum) = prod (1-x)^(-c/2)  =>  exp(2 sum) = 1/prod_terms
   961	    exp2sum_fac = factor_frac(1 / prod_terms)
   962	    closed_match = fac_eq(exp2sum_fac, PAPER["ratio_const_sq"])
   963	    mpmath.mp.dps = 50
   964	    partial = mpmath.fsum(mpmath.mpf(4) ** k / (2 * k) * (mpmath.mpf(15) ** -k + 2 * mpmath.mpf(-5) ** -k
   965	                                                          + 2 * mpmath.mpf(-3) ** -k) ** 2
   966	                          for k in range(1, 401))
   967	    closed_val = mpmath.log(mpmath.mpf(3) ** 13 * mpmath.mpf(5) ** 13
   968	                            / (mpmath.mpf(7) ** 6 * mpmath.mpf(11) ** 4 * mpmath.mpf(79) ** 2
   969	                               * mpmath.sqrt(13 * 17)))
   970	    item5["b_sum_lambda_delta_sq_(2.3)"] = {
   971	        "source": "recomputed: series expansion, -1/2 ln(1-x) identity, exact product",
   972	        "lambda_k_delta_k^2 == (1/2k) sum c x^k (6 geometric terms), k=1..40": expand_ok,
   973	        "paper_intermediate_product_equals_prod_(1-x)^c": paper_inner == prod_terms,
   974	        "prod_(1-x)^c_factorisation": fac_str(factor_frac(prod_terms)),
   975	        "exp(2*sum)_factorisation": fac_str(exp2sum_fac),
   976	        "paper_(2.3)_squared": fac_str(PAPER["ratio_const_sq"]),
   977	        "verdict": verdict(expand_ok and paper_inner == prod_terms and closed_match),
   978	        "numeric_cross_check_mpmath": {
   979	            "partial_sum_k<=400": mpmath.nstr(partial, 30),
   980	            "ln(3^13 5^13/(7^6 11^4 79^2 sqrt(221)))": mpmath.nstr(closed_val, 30),
   981	            "abs_diff": mpmath.nstr(abs(partial - closed_val), 5),
   982	            "exp_value": mpmath.nstr(mpmath.exp(closed_val), 20),
   983	        },
   984	    }
   985	    # (c) (2.4) from Lemma 3.1 via Stirling (recomputed), saddle-point variance recomputed
   986	    coeffs = {1: 5, 2: 10, 3: 10, 4: 5}                 # (x+1)^5 - x^5 - 1
   987	    tot = sum(coeffs.values())
   988	    mean = Fraction(sum(k * c for k, c in coeffs.items()), tot)
   989	    var2 = Fraction(sum(k * k * c for k, c in coeffs.items()), tot) - mean ** 2
   990	    # N ~ 30^(n/3) / sqrt(2 pi (n/3) var2) = c_N (pi n)^(-1/2) 30^(n/3) with c_N^2 = 3/(2 var2)
   991	    cN_sq = Fraction(3) / (2 * var2)
   992	    multinom = a_stirling(1) / a_stirling(Fraction(1, 3)) ** 3
   993	    P_size = a_stirling(5) / (a_base(2, Fraction(5, 2)) * a_stirling(Fraction(5, 2)))
   994	    A_n = (a_const(3) * a_const(2, Fraction(1, 2)) * a_const(11, Fraction(-1, 2))
   995	           * Asym(pi_e=Fraction(-1, 2), n_e=Fraction(-1, 2)) * a_base(30, Fraction(1, 3))) ** 3
   996	    EY = multinom * a_stirling(Fraction(5, 6)) ** 3 / P_size * A_n
   997	    C1, k1, B1 = EY.normalised()
   998	    EY_match = (fac_eq(fac_scale(C1, 2), PAPER["EY_const_sq"]) and k1 == -1
   999	                and fac_eq(fac_scale(B1, 2), factor_frac(Fraction(25, 24))))
  1000	    # exact finite-n EY (Lemma 3.1 proof, exact formula) vs (2.4): ratio -> 1 (numeric)
  1001	    # N = [x^(5n/6)] (5x(1+x)(1+x+x^2))^(n/3) = 5^a sum_k C(a, n/2-k) [x^k](1+x+x^2)^a,  a = n/3
  1002	    def coeff_N(n):
  1003	        a = n // 3
  1004	        binp = flint.fmpz_poly([1, 1]) ** a
  1005	        trip = flint.fmpz_poly([1, 1, 1]) ** a
  1006	        return 5 ** a * sum(int(binp[n // 2 - kk]) * int(trip[kk])
  1007	                            for kk in range(max(0, n // 2 - a), min(2 * a, n // 2) + 1))
  1008	
  1009	    mpmath.mp.dps = 60
  1010	    finite = []
  1011	    for n in (60, 600, 6000, 24000):
  1012	        a = n // 3
  1013	        Nn = coeff_N(n)
  1014	        logEY = (mpmath.loggamma(n + 1) - 3 * mpmath.loggamma(a + 1) + 3 * mpmath.loggamma(5 * n // 6 + 1)
  1015	                 + 3 * mpmath.log(mpmath.mpf(Nn))
  1016	                 - (mpmath.loggamma(5 * n + 1) - mpmath.mpf(5 * n) / 2 * mpmath.log(2)
  1017	                    - mpmath.loggamma(mpmath.mpf(5 * n) / 2 + 1)))
  1018	        logasym = (mpmath.log(mpmath.sqrt(mpmath.mpf(2 ** 2 * 3 ** 6 * 5 ** 3) / 11 ** 3))
  1019	                   - mpmath.log(2 * mpmath.pi * n) + mpmath.mpf(n) / 2 * mpmath.log(mpmath.mpf(25) / 24))
  1020	        ratio = mpmath.exp(logEY - logasym)
  1021	        finite.append({"n": n, "EY_exact/EY_(2.4)": mpmath.nstr(ratio, 15),
  1022	                       "n*(ratio-1)": mpmath.nstr(n * (ratio - 1), 10)})
  1023	    # check the coefficient extraction identity once exactly at small n
  1024	    n_small = 60
  1025	    direct = (flint.fmpz_poly([0, 5, 10, 10, 5]) ** (n_small // 3))[5 * n_small // 6]
  1026	    via = coeff_N(n_small)
  1027	    item5["c_EY_(2.4)"] = {
  1028	        "source": "recomputed from Lemma 3.1 (statement and exact formula EY = multinomial (5n/6)!^3 N^3/|P_n5|) "
  1029	                  "with Stirling; saddle-point variance recomputed",
  1030	        "saddle_mean_per_vertex": str(mean),
  1031	        "saddle_variance_per_vertex": str(var2),
  1032	        "c_N^2 = 3/(2 var) (paper: (3 sqrt2/sqrt11)^2 = 18/11)": str(cN_sq),
  1033	        "c_N_matches_paper": cN_sq == Fraction(18, 11),
  1034	        "EY_asymptotic_recomputed": EY.describe(),
  1035	        "EY_const_squared": fac_str(fac_scale(C1, 2)),
  1036	        "paper_(2.4)_const_squared": fac_str(PAPER["EY_const_sq"]),
  1037	        "verdict": verdict(EY_match and cN_sq == Fraction(18, 11)),
  1038	        "coefficient_identity_N_(n=60)_exact": int(direct) == via,
  1039	        "numeric_cross_check_exact_finite_n": finite,
  1040	    }
  1041	    # (d) h(n) of Lemma 5.1 from (4.7) via Stirling (d-independent part; sum d = 1, sum m = 5)
  1042	    h_rec = (a_base(2, Fraction(5, 2)) * a_stirling(Fraction(5, 2)) / a_stirling(5) * a_stirling(1)
  1043	             * a_two_pi_n(-162) * a_n_over_e(-1)          # 1/prod_324 (d n)!: d-independent part
  1044	             * a_two_pi_n(9) * a_n_over_e(Fraction(5, 2)))  # prod_36 ((m n)!)^(1/2): d-independent part
  1045	    h_paper = a_const(2, Fraction(-1, 2)) * a_two_pi_n(PAPER["h_two_pi_n_exp"]) * a_base(5, Fraction(-5, 2))
  1046	    item5["d_h(n)_Lemma_5.1"] = {
  1047	        "source": "recomputed from (4.7): Stirling for n!, (5n/2)!, (5n)!, 324 factorials (d n)! and "
  1048	                  "36 factorials (m n)!^(1/2); the d-dependent leftovers are exactly g(d) e^(n f(d))",
  1049	        "h_recomputed": h_rec.describe(),
  1050	        "h_paper": h_paper.describe(),
  1051	        "verdict": verdict(h_rec == h_paper),
  1052	    }
  1053	    # (e) Lemma 5.9 constant and (2.5) from the exact det and g (Laplace + Euler-Maclaurin, method
  1054	    #     taken from the paper, constants recomputed); conditional on the Maximum Hypothesis.
  1055	    absdet_fac = detH_fac
  1056	    lap = Asym(const={2: Fraction(NFREE, 2)}, pi_e=Fraction(NFREE, 2), n_e=Fraction(NFREE, 2))  # n^301 (2pi/n)^(301/2)
  1057	    L59 = (lap * Asym(const=g_fac) * Asym(const=fac_scale(absdet_fac, Fraction(-1, 2)))
  1058	           * a_base(5, Fraction(5, 2)) * a_base(Fraction(25, 24)))
  1059	    L59_paper = (Asym(const=fac_scale(PAPER["L59_const_sq"], Fraction(1, 2))) * a_two_pi_n(Fraction(NFREE, 2))
  1060	                 * a_base(Fraction(25, 24)) * a_base(5, Fraction(5, 2)))
  1061	    EY2 = h_rec * L59
  1062	    C2, k2, B2 = EY2.normalised()
  1063	    EY2_paper_fac = fac_scale(PAPER["EY2_const_sq"], Fraction(1, 2))
  1064	    EY2_match = fac_eq(C2, EY2_paper_fac) and k2 == -2 and fac_eq(B2, factor_frac(Fraction(25, 24)))
  1065	    ratio = EY2 / (EY ** 2)
  1066	    Cr, kr, Br = ratio.normalised()
  1067	    ratio_match_paper = fac_eq(fac_scale(Cr, 2), PAPER["ratio_const_sq"]) and kr == 0 and Br == {}
  1068	    ratio_eq_exp_sum = fac_eq(fac_scale(Cr, 2), exp2sum_fac) and kr == 0 and Br == {}
  1069	    item5["e_Lemma_5.9_and_(2.5)"] = {
  1070	        "source": "Laplace/Euler-Maclaurin structure (5.25)-(5.27) taken from the paper; g(b), |det H~| "
  1071	                  "recomputed above; CONDITIONAL on the Maximum Hypothesis (not checked here)",

def rank(a):
 z=[list(map(F,r)) for r in a];p=0

class I:
 def __init__(self,x):
  if isinstance(x,F): self.l=cl.divide(D(x.numerator),D(x.denominator));self.h=ch.divide(D(x.numerator),D(x.denominator))

  z=object.__new__(I);z.l=l;z.h=h;return z
 @staticmethod

 def __mul__(self,x):

def nine(v):

def midpoint(a,b):

 z=tuple((v<<(h-d))+(w<<(h-e)) for v,w in zip(x,y));h+=1
